### [1] [Theatre Chapbooks At Scale: A Statistical Comparative Analysis of Typography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27266)
*Diego Belzarena,Seginus Mowlavi,Paula Casariego Castiñeira,Alejandra Ulla Lorenzo,Gregory Randall,Jean-Michel Morel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统计方法，用于量化历史图书中字体的相似性，可大规模、自动化辅助文献学分析，已应用于17世纪西班牙戏剧小册子的字体归属，获得了实际成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工进行历史字体归属和印刷厂识别依赖专家主观视觉判断，人力成本高且难以大规模开展，因此亟需自动化、客观的分析工具。

Method: 自动提取图书内的字符图像，通过聚类和对齐获得字体原型，然后计算任意两本书的字体距离，并引入a contrario统计框架评估距离显著性，实现字体相似度的量化分析。

Result: 方法已成功应用于17世纪西班牙戏剧小册子的罗马体与斜体印刷字体归属分析，结果经专家验证可靠，并修正与发现了部分新的印刷厂归属。

Conclusion: 本方法能够大幅提升文献字体分析的效率与准确性，为数字书志、历史文献研究等提供大规模自动化的技术基础，具有广泛的推广应用价值。

Abstract: We propose a statistical methodology that quantifies the similarity of typefaces between printed historical books. This provides a tool that accelerates philological analysis. Using character prototypes derived from clustering and aligning automatically extracted character images, the method defines a typeface distance between any two books. To produce actionable outputs, we develop an a contrario statistical framework to interpret the significance of the computed typeface distances. We apply the method to the philological study of 17 th -century Spanish printed theatre chapbooks in a quantity that exceeds the capabilities of systematic visual inspection by human experts. Our method enables the automatic comparison of Roman and Italic types extracted from different books. After validation by human experts, our method has led to new printer attributions being discovered, and former printer attributions being revised. This success strongly suggests that our method has the potential to enable digital bibliography on a larger scale than was previously possible.

</details>


### [2] [OVEarth-Bench: Evaluating Category Breadth and Query Diversity for Open-Vocabulary Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27278)
*Kaiyu Li,Zepeng Xin,Zixuan Jiang,Jing Fu,Lanxuan Xue,Lingyu Zhang,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个新基准OVEarth-Bench，提升地球观测领域开放词汇定位任务的多样性和广度，系统评测多种方法，发现一般方法优于专用方法，提高未来研究参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇地球观测任务的评测基准受限于类别和查询的狭窄范围，不能全面反映方法的能力，需要更具广度和多样性的基准推动领域进步。

Method: 提出OVEarth-Bench，扩展类别层次与表达方式，包括正负表达、语义推理等多样化查询，统一掩码和框的定位评测协议，在零样本设置下评估多种通用与地球观测特定方法的性能。

Result: 1）现有方法性能有限，类别扩宽有助于稳定排名；2）MLLM类通用模型表现最佳；3）地球观测专用方法多数不如通用模型，鲜有能达到最优。

Conclusion: 本基准为开放词汇地球观测任务设计和方法改进提供了方向，强调了构建真实、多样、高质和大规模基准的重要意义。数据和评测工具已开放。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary Earth observation (EO) aims to localize geospatial concepts specified in natural language rather than a fixed label set. Existing benchmarks, however, usually cover narrow category vocabularies or limited query forms. To fill this gap, we introduce OVEarth-Bench, which extends existing evaluation in two directions: category breadth, through broad hierarchical category coverage with positive and negative expressions, and query diversity, through vocabulary, referring, and reasoning queries. The benchmark supports mask and box localization under a unified zero-shot protocol. We evaluate a broad set of general and EO-specific methods. The evaluation reveals that: (1) the performance of current methods remains limited, while broader category coverage yields more stable model rankings; (2) MLLM-based methods achieve the strongest overall performance; and (3) EO-specific methods generally underperform general models and rarely match the strongest methods. These findings provide guidance for future open-vocabulary EO method design and highlight the importance of developing more realistic, diverse, high-quality, and large-scale benchmarks for reliable evaluation. Our data and evaluation package are released at https://earth-insights.github.io/OVEarth-bench.

</details>


### [3] [VETO: Towards Protecting Images From Frontier AI Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27292)
*Jonas Grebe,Hossein Shakibania,Tobias Braun,Marcus Rohrbach,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的抗深度编辑防护方法VETO，以及配套的测试基准VetoBench，可有效抑制高自由度图片编辑模型的非授权篡改行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，诸如FLUX.2等强大的图像编辑模型快速普及，其能力已扩展到在新场景中提取和重构对象，极大提升了编辑自由度。但与此同时，也带来了更广泛的误用风险，现有的防护方法无法有效阻止新型模型的编辑能力，亟需更有效的安全防护手段。

Method: 本文分析了现代图像编辑模型利用联合注意力机制绕过旧有防护措施的问题，提出了VETO：一种通过干扰模型读取源图像机制的、微妙的反编辑保护“斗篷”，并引入了VetoBench基准，专门针对传统局部编辑和新型情景化变换两类威胁进行防护性能评估。

Result: VETO在两种主流编辑模型和三项基准下均显著优于现有反编辑防护方法，实现了更佳的保护与图像保真度折中。

Conclusion: VETO及VetoBench为应对高度灵活图像编辑模型可能带来的滥用提供了新思路，新方法能有效防护来自现代模型的复杂篡改行为，优于当前已有解决方案。

Abstract: The rise of powerful, accessible image-editing models such as FLUX.2 has brought high-fidelity editing within broad reach. Their capabilities now extend beyond localized modifications to extracting and recontextualizing objects and identities in entirely new scenes. By allowing prompt and generation tokens to attend directly to reference-image tokens, modern models blur the boundary between conventional editing and text-to-image synthesis. This expanded generative freedom also broadens the space of potential misuse, as harmful transformations are no longer confined to a predictable set of localized edits. Existing anti-edit defenses are designed to disrupt the semantic bottleneck of the reference-image encoding in legacy diffusion pipelines. However, newer editors distill reference information through joint-attention blocks, thereby often circumventing these protections. We therefore introduce VETO, a subtle anti-edit cloak that disrupts this inner mechanism through which modern models read the source image. Additionally, as existing editing benchmarks leave comprehensive recontextualizations largely untested, we introduce VetoBench, which evaluates defenses not only on conventional localized edits but also on broader contextual shifts. Across two contemporary editing models and three benchmarks, VETO consistently outperforms existing defenses while providing a stronger protection-fidelity trade-off.

</details>


### [4] [Bunraku: Turning a Single Illustration into an Editable Live2D Character](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27348)
*Junhao Chen,Jingjia Mao,Dayong Li,Chenghai Li,Saining Zhang,Zhihao Li,Hao Zhao,Yufei Wang,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了首个能从单幅插画自动生成完整Live2D模型的系统，显著降低了模型制作门槛，并发布了标准数据集和评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Live2D在虚拟主播、手游等领域被广泛使用，现有Live2D模型的制作依赖繁琐且耗时的手工操作（如分层、补全遮挡、网格布置及关键帧动画），缺乏端到端自动化方法，极大限制了其普及和生产力。

Method: 方法分两阶段：第一阶段基于Live2D器官级分类将分层分解为扩散模型问题，输出有序的RGBA分层并完成被遮挡区域；第二阶段仅利用alpha通道为每层生成网格，联动预测各层位移场，实现协调的角色动画。所有层的顶点联合建模，提升了整体一致性，突破了之前分层独立预测的局限性。还构建了Live2D-Bench标准评测和大规模标注数据集。

Result: 在50个未见角色上，无教师强制的真实生成下，第二阶段达到了每顶点方向余弦0.768（中位数0.828）的指标。利用分层网格，角色服装层可用自然语言重新着色且动画可无需修改直接复用。

Conclusion: 本研究首次实现了从插画到可用Live2D模型的自动化生成流程，极大降低制作门槛，推动了Live2D资产的规模化生产和应用。标准数据集和评测推动了该方向的进一步研究。

Abstract: Live2D is the dominant 2D character-animation format for anime characters and virtual avatars, representing each character as a stack of RGBA layers driven by per-layer mesh deformation. Despite its wide use in virtual streaming, mobile games, and interactive characters, authoring a Live2D model still demands weeks of manual layer separation, occlusion completion, mesh placement, and keyframing, and no prior generative method produces such a structured asset end-to-end. We present the first system that, from a single illustration, generates all the structured information a Live2D runtime consumes: ordered RGBA layers, a deformation mesh per layer, and the parameter-driven keypose vertex offsets that make the character move. Stage 1 casts layered decomposition as a layered diffusion process under a Live2D-aware organ-level taxonomy, producing an ordered RGBA stack with hidden-region completion. Stage 2 builds a content-conforming triangle mesh for each layer from its alpha channel alone, then predicts the keypose displacement field of all layers jointly: every vertex of every layer is one token, self-attention spans layer boundaries, and each displacement is factorised into a bounded direction and a log-magnitude. Joint rather than independent prediction is what makes the result a coherent character instead of separately plausible parts, and is our largest gain; scaling the network 112x yields none. On 50 held-out characters, under true generation with no teacher forcing, Stage 2 attains a per-vertex direction cosine of 0.768 (median 0.828). Because a layer's mesh derives from its alpha channel, a clothing layer can be re-textured from a natural-language instruction while the mesh and predicted animation are reused byte-for-byte. We further contribute Live2D-Bench, the first standardized benchmark for the task, and an 8,884-model Live2D corpus with layer and animation supervision.

</details>


### [5] [Shared Semantic Codebook Distillation for Unpaired Cross-Modal Medical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27357)
*Dillan Imans,Phuoc-Nguyen Bui,Duc-Tai Le,Hyunseung Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于医学影像分析中跨模态无配对场景的知识蒸馏方法——SSCD，通过共享的语义码本在全局和类别条件下对齐教师与学生的特征分布，实现无须样本配对的知识迁移，显著提升了学生模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像中不同模态（如OCT与fundus, CT与X-ray）往往来自不同病人，特征空间不兼容，传统实例级知识蒸馏方法难以应用，急需解决无配对情况下的跨模态知识迁移问题。

Method: 提出Shared Semantic Codebook Distillation (SSCD)方法。将每幅图像编码为共享、模态无关的离散语义词汇分布，通过对齐这种分布实现知识迁移。码本使用指数滑动平均方法在线更新，通过熵正则化和死亡码重启机制保持多样性。训练后推理阶段仅保留学生端编码器和分类器，不增加额外开销。

Result: 在OCT到fundus的视网膜疾病分类和CT到X-ray的肺炎分类两个异构无配对任务中，学生模型性能分别从64.5提升到70.2、从73.8提升到76.3的macro-F1，均优于所有已评估的蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: SSCD有效解决了医学影像无配对跨模态知识蒸馏难题，实现了跨模态特征分布的无监督对齐，显著增强了低成本模态下学生模型的诊断性能。

Abstract: Cross-modal knowledge distillation can transfer diagnostic knowledge from a strong but costly teacher modality to a cheaper and more deployable student modality. In medical image analysis, however, the two modalities are often unpaired: they are collected from different patient cohorts and occupy geometrically incompatible feature spaces. This makes instance-level distillation invalid and direct feature matching unreliable. To address these challenges, we propose Shared Semantic Codebook Distillation (SSCD), which compares teacher and student representations through a shared discrete codebook. Each image is represented as a distribution over a common, modality-agnostic vocabulary, and knowledge is transferred by aligning these distributions across modalities, both globally and class-conditionally, without requiring paired samples or directly comparable raw features. The codebook is evolved online by exponential moving average and kept diverse through entropy regularization and dead-code restart. At inference, all teacher-side and codebook modules are discarded, leaving only the student encoder and classifier. On two heterogeneous unpaired settings, OCT-to-fundus retinal disease classification and CT-to-chest-X-ray pneumonia classification, SSCD improves the student from 64.5 to 70.2 macro-F1 and from 73.8 to 76.3 macro-F1, respectively, outperforming all evaluated distillation baselines on both settings. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/DillanImans/SSCD-unpaired-distillation

</details>


### [6] [PanDent: Toward Comprehensive Tooth-Level Structure-Language Consistency in Dental Radiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27378)
*Xiaohan Li,Xinyu Liu,Chang Liu,Sum Wing Au Yeung,Jun Liu,Yixuan Yuan,Hui Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个临床可靠的口腔全景片（OPG）细粒度基准数据集PanDent，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在牙科图像诊断中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在牙科全景片诊断领域缺乏细致、权威的评估基准，难以反映专家的解读能力，影响其实际临床应用价值。

Method: 作者构建了9,524张高质量口腔全景片数据集，所有数据均为资深牙医细粒度牙齿级别标注，并由口腔颌面放射学专家复核。通过临床专家定义的报告逻辑，将结构化临床证据与自由文本描述精确对应，实现高可信度的报告生成和评测体系。

Result: 实验证明，当前多模态大模型虽能生成流畅语言，但细致定位和诊断一致性存在明显不足。基于PanDent数据集微调后，模型的结构化诊断能力和视觉定位能力大幅提升，报告更贴合真实专家解读。

Conclusion: PanDent为MLLMs在牙齿级临床推理提供了权威评测基准，有助于推进临床可靠的牙科AI研究。

Abstract: Accurate evaluation of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in dental panoramic radiography (orthopantomogram, OPG) is limited by the lack of fine-grained, clinically reliable benchmarks that reflect expert interpretation. This work introduces PanDent, a large-scale, clinically grounded OPG benchmark built upon fine-grained, expert-validated tooth-level annotations. The dataset comprises 9,524 high-quality OPGs, each associated with comprehensive structured annotations produced by experienced dentists and further validated by an oral and maxillofacial radiologist, providing clinically reliable supervision for tooth-level diagnosis and reasoning. Clinically consistent radiology reports are constructed from expert-validated findings using clinician-defined reporting logic, establishing explicit correspondence between structured clinical evidence and free-text descriptions. This design enables evaluation of whether MLLMs generate reports that are not only linguistically coherent but also clinically consistent with expert-validated tooth-level findings. Experiments are conducted on diverse MLLMs, including state-of-the-art (SOTA) proprietary models, general-domain open-source models, and medical-specific models. Results show that current MLLMs can generate fluent reports, yet fail to produce clinically consistent descriptions, exhibiting substantial errors in fine-grained localization and tooth-level diagnosis. Fine-tuning on PanDent significantly improves structure-language consistency, substantially enhancing visual localization accuracy and diagnostic correctness, and bringing model outputs closer to expert dental interpretation. These results establish PanDent as a rigorous benchmark for evaluating tooth-level clinical reasoning in MLLMs and a valuable resource for clinically grounded dental AI.

</details>


### [7] [VideoCoCo: Code-as-CoT for Physically-Consistent Video Generation via an Agentic Dual-Engine System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27380)
*Haodong Li,Tianfei Ren,Xiaoxiao Ma,Chunmei Qing,Zhen Fang,Sipeng He,Ziyu Guo,Haoyu Wu,Juanxi Tian,Yihang Zou,Ruichuan An,Dongzhi Jiang,Boxue Yang,Ji Xie,Xu Huang,Wenhao Yan,Jialv Zou,Zhengrong Yue,Yaxin Luo,Xiaotong Li,Yuzhu Wang,Junyan Ye,Jinjing Zhao,Zehui Chen,Lin Chen,Renye Yan,Feng Zhao,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoCoCo，通过将可执行的Blender代码作为过程级Chain of Thought，实现更物理一致的文本到视频生成，在多个基准上取得了最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到视频模型难以生成物理一致的动态场景，因为大部分模型需要从高度压缩的文本提示中隐式推断场景演变，且现有的思维链（Chain of Thought）方案缺乏可执行性或时间维度稀疏，限制了时空过程的精细控制。

Method: 提出VideoCoCo框架。首先由代码生成代理根据文本提示生成Blender程序，显式描述场景和时间演化。该程序由可执行仿真引擎运行，产生可控的时空草稿，再通过生成式视频引擎将草稿转化为高质量视频。为适配编辑器，作者构建了VideoCoCo-3K数据集。

Result: VideoCoCo在PhyGenBench基准上将OmniWeaving的分数从0.475提升到0.558，在VBench-2.0上从52.18提升到77.88，两个基准上都取得了最佳平均分。

Conclusion: 可执行代码为物理一致的视频生成提供了可控、可检查的中间表征，显著提升了生成质量。

Abstract: Text-to-video models have achieved remarkable visual quality, yet they still struggle to generate physically consistent dynamics because the temporal evolution of a scene must be inferred implicitly from a highly compressed text prompt. Existing chain-of-thought approaches introduce intermediate plans or visual states, but these representations are typically non-executable or temporally sparse, limiting their ability to instantiate and control the complete spatiotemporal process. To address this limitation, we introduce VideoCoCo, an agentic dual-engine framework in which executable Blender code serves as a process-level chain of thought. Given a text prompt, a coding agent synthesizes a Blender program that explicitly specifies the scene and its temporal evolution. The executable simulation engine runs the program to produce a deterministic spatiotemporal draft, which is subsequently transformed into a photorealistic video by a generative video engine through draft-conditioned editing. This decomposition separates process-level reasoning from high-fidelity visual realization. To adapt the video editor to simulated drafts, we construct VideoCoCo-3K, a curated dataset of draft-instruction-target triplets. VideoCoCo improves the OmniWeaving baseline from 0.475 to 0.558 on PhyGenBench and from 52.18 to 77.88 on VBench-2.0, achieving the best average score on both benchmarks. These results demonstrate that executable code provides an effective, controllable, and inspectable intermediate representation for physically consistent video generation.

</details>


### [8] [IGME: Efficient Chained Method Ensemble for Transferable Semantic Segmentation Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27465)
*Mengqi He,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的单源可迁移攻击方法（IGME），可在仅需一个源模型的情况下，对语义分割模型实施可迁移对抗攻击，取得了对比基线有竞争力的攻击效果及出色的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗迁移性攻击方法在密集预测任务如语义分割中，往往依赖于多个代理模型进行集成攻击，计算资源消耗大，难以应用。为此，作者希望设计一个更高效、只需单一源模型即可实现的可迁移攻击方法。

Method: 作者将可迁移攻击的生成过程表述为可微攻击组件的链式组合，从而实现对代价高昂的源模型梯度计算的重用。为降低链式组合带来的更新不稳定性，引入了集成梯度风格的路径平均方向作为稳定性经验启发方法（IGME）。

Result: 在Pascal VOC和Cityscapes这两个数据集上，针对基于CNN及Transformer的分割模型进行了实验。结果显示，IGME方法在只访问一个源模型的条件下，可获得与单源基线相当的迁移攻击能力，并且在计算耗时上明显优于需要多个模型的集成攻击方法。

Conclusion: IGME方法有效且高效地实现了对语义分割模型的转移性攻击，兼具攻击性能与计算效率，证明了链式攻击和路径平均策略在实际中的实用价值，且极大地降低了对资源的需求。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation models are vulnerable to transferable adversarial perturbations, yet evaluating transfer attacks on dense prediction models can be computationally expensive. Existing ensemble attacks often rely on multiple surrogate models, increasing the computation cost, even harder for segmentation. This paper studies an efficient single-source alternative for transferable attacks on semantic segmentation. We formulate transferable attack composition as a chained computation over differentiable attack components, allowing the expensive source-model gradient computation to be shared. To reduce the update instability introduced by chained composition, we further use an integrated-gradient-style path-averaged direction as an empirical stabilization heuristic. Experiments on Pascal VOC and Cityscapes evaluate the resulting transferability efficiency trade-off across CNN- and transformer-based segmentation models. IGME achieves competitive transferability compared with single-source baselines and favorable runtime compared with model-ensemble attacks, while requiring access to only one source model.

</details>


### [9] [ProgFormer: Hierarchical Voxel Diffusion Transformer for Longitudinal Brain MRI Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27537)
*Dexuan Ding,Yuankai Qi,Luping Zhou,Jian Yang,Quan Z. Sheng,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层的体素空间扩散Transformer模型ProgFormer，能够更好地预测脑部MRI未来结构，兼顾全局结构一致性和对细微疾病进展变化的敏感性，并在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测脑部结构MRI未来变化具有挑战，因脑部大部分结构随时间稳定，仅有部分区域呈现细微变化。现有方法要么因潜空间重构信息损失，要么局部细微变化被全局结构淹没，难以兼顾整体与细节。

Method: 作者提出ProgFormer，采用分层结构：粗路径通过3D patch tokens建模整体结构和长期信息，细路径在粗路径引导下精细预测patch内部的体素级变化。两者共同直接在体素空间估计速度场，实现端到端无编码器的预测，通过Euler积分生成最终预测图像。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL、OASIS三个主流数据库上的配对与轨迹预测任务中，ProgFormer在多项指标上均优于多种现有主流方法。

Conclusion: ProgFormer能有效兼顾全局一致性与细节变化敏感性，为纵向脑部MRI预测任务提供了新的有效工具，具有广泛实际应用前景。

Abstract: Predicting future structural MRI of a brain is challenging because longitudinal changes are often subtle and confined to specific anatomical regions, while most subject-specific brain structure remains stable over time. An effective model should therefore preserve global brain structural consistency while remaining sensitive to fine-grained disease progression. Existing latent-space-based methods improve computational efficiency, but suffer from information loss during their compression-reconstruction procedure. In contrast, direct voxel-space methods avoid latent reconstruction but commonly use a unified prediction pathway to model brain structure and progression-related changes. Subtle local changes may therefore be overshadowed by the dominant stable brain structure. To address these challenges, we propose ProgFormer, a hierarchical voxel-space Diffusion Transformer for longitudinal brain MRI prediction. ProgFormer uses a coarse pathway to perform the primary volumetric prediction from 3D patch tokens. This pathway models overall brain structure and longitudinal context. The fine pathway then uses the coarse representations as spatio-temporal grounding for voxel-level refinement within individual patches. The two pathways jointly estimate a velocity field directly in voxel space through conditional flow matching, enabling end-to-end prediction without a separately learned image autoencoder. The predicted future scan is then generated from Gaussian noise by integrating the estimated velocity field over a sequence of Euler steps. Extensive experimental results on three widely used benchmarks, ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS, under both pairwise and trajectory settings demonstrate favourable performance compared against several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [10] [Drawing-Recode: Annotation Grounding for Parametric CAD Code Generation from Raster 2D CAD Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27558)
*Mingi Kim,Yongjun Kim,Hyungki Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新方法Drawing-Recode，将光栅化2D CAD图纸自动转换为参数化CAD代码，提升了旧图纸数字化和制造自动化的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旧设备和工厂积累了大量纸质或扫描的2D CAD光栅图纸，参数化信息缺失，无法自动化用于制造和零件复现。现有方法要么只能处理矢量图纸，要么仅适用于特定领域，且无法将尺寸注释与几何信息有效关联，限制了参数化三维CAD重建的自动化程度。

Method: 提出Drawing-Recode框架，首先通过图像编码器提取图像中几何特征，再用文本识别模块识别注释，接着借助交叉注意力机制和提出的注释锚定损失（AGL）将注释与几何信息显式绑定，最后将全部特征输入大型语言模型，生成结构化参数化CAD代码（SPCC）。

Result: 实验证明，Drawing-Recode在参数化CAD代码生成任务上优于现有基线方法，且在接近工业常见条件（如扫描图纸）的测试中表现出较高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Drawing-Recode有望推动工业领域光栅化2D CAD图纸的数字化，提升零件复现与制造过程的自动化水平。

Abstract: Recovering Parametric CAD sequences from raster-format 2D Computer-Aided Design (CAD) drawings accumulated prior to digital transformation is important for part reproduction and manufacturing process automation. However, existing studies either process only vector drawings or are limited to specific domains, and fail to explicitly connect dimensional annotations to geometric information, limiting their use of dimensional information for 3D Parametric CAD sequences recovery. We propose Drawing-Recode, a framework that generates Parametric CAD sequences as CAD code from raster 2D CAD drawings. Drawing-Recode extracts geometric features via an image encoder and recognizes annotations through a separate text recognition module, then explicitly grounds annotations to geometric information using cross-attention and our proposed Annotation Grounding Loss (AGL). The resulting features are fed into a Large Language Model (LLM) to generate CAD code in the Structured Parametric CAD Code (SPCC) format. Experiments show that Drawing-Recode outperforms existing baselines and remains robust on scanned drawings resembling industrial conditions. We expect Drawing-Recode contributes to digitizing raster 2D CAD drawings in industrial settings and to part reproduction and manufacturing automation.

</details>


### [11] [Inference-Time Agentic Decision Rules Beat Longer Evolving Search for Multi-Image Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27564)
*Site Li,Jianyi Hao,Xiaofeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多图像医学视觉问答（VQA）中的决策机制，发现简单的order-vote策略在鲁棒性和准确率上优于更复杂的方法，并且优化搜索预算的增加对泛化能力提升有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多图像医学VQA不仅仅是提示长度的问题，更是关于如何进行高效且鲁棒决策的核心挑战，因此需要系统比较不同的推理策略。

Method: 作者设计了受控实验，对五种推理时的智能体决策策略进行对比，全部采用相同的高预算ShinkaEvolve配置，在内部数据集上进行多次独立实验评估，比较其准确率和泛化能力。

Result: order-vote（排序投票）这一简单策略在最终测试准确率（57.89%）上显著优于固定基线（52.73%）和更复杂但易受干扰的order-rerank（55.79%），扩展进化搜索代数对最终测试精度无益，甚至略有下降。

Conclusion: 对于多图像医学推理场景，选择合适且鲁棒的决策规则比一味提升优化预算更为关键，优雅简单的策略也可以带来更强泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-image medical VQA is not merely a prompt-length problem; it is a fundamental challenge of agentic decision-making. Medical vision-language agents must aggregate evidence across ordered images, remain robust to answer-order perturbations, and avoid overfitting to noisy search-time feedback. We study MedFrameQA through a controlled comparison of five inference-time agentic strategies, optimized using the same high-budget ShinkaEvolve configuration and evaluated on a reproducible internal frozen split (1,331 evolution, 665 holdout, 855 final test). Across five independent repeated runs, the strongest method emerges as the simplest robust aggregator: the \textbf{order-vote} policy achieves $57.89 \pm 0.65\%$ final-test accuracy, significantly outperforming the fixed baseline ($52.73 \pm 0.42\%$) and the more complex, albeit brittle, order-rerank variant ($55.79 \pm 0.43\%$). Paired bootstrap analysis confirms these significant gains. Extending the evolutionary search budget from 50 to 100 generations yields no generalization benefit: while holdout performance marginally increases, final-test accuracy drops from $57.89\%$ to $56.02\%$. Our findings suggest that for multi-image medical reasoning, defining the correct agentic decision rule is substantially more impactful than expanding the optimization search budget.

</details>


### [12] [Objective-Aligned Direct Answer SFT for Robust Multi-Frame Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27566)
*Site Li,Jianyi Hao,Xiaofeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文研究了多帧医学VQA任务的不同模型适配方式，发现直接以目标答案为导向的微调(SFT)方法在稳定性和准确性上优于复杂的辅助机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在多帧医学VQA领域中，模型适配常用复杂的控制、重排序、混合监督等机制，这些方法本质上提升了任务表现，但是否真的必需、是否存在更简单而鲁棒的方案尚未明确。

Method: 论文以MedFrameQA数据集为例，控制变量（如数据划分、计算预算、随机种子等）对比了多种模型：包含控制器、脚手架进化、混合监管、递进式微调以及直接以答案为目标的SFT微调。并评估了后处理校准的效果及方法在不同主干网络（如Qwen2.5-VL-3B）上的迁移能力。

Result: 实验发现，直接以目标答案SFT微调的模型在MedGemma-1.5-4B上表现最强，较冷启动基线显著提升，且在多次重复实验和严格控制下表现鲁棒。此外，事后校准方法能有效提高置信度估计而不损害准确率。该方法也稳定迁移到其他骨干模型。

Conclusion: 复杂的机制未能超越简单直接的目标对齐式SFT微调。论文为医学多视觉问答提供了鲁棒、简约的基线，有助于社区将关注点转向更本质的鲁棒优化，而非架构复杂度。

Abstract: Multi-frame medical VQA appears to reward increasingly complex adaptation: controller-style inference, localization-aware reranking, static hard-negative mixing, and staged continuation all appear plausible from first principles. We test a simpler competing hypothesis on MedFrameQA: methods that remain tightly aligned with the benchmark's final answer objective should be the strongest \emph{robust} adaptation family once evaluation is controlled across fixed splits, matched budgets, repeated seeds, and calibration. We compare controller-based methods, scaffold evolution, static mixed supervision, continuation-heavy variants, and direct answer-only supervised fine-tuning (SFT). The strongest robust family is direct decoder-only answer SFT on MedGemma-1.5-4B. Empirically, this family yields substantial improvements in held-out report accuracy over frozen baselines while remaining remarkably stable across repeated seeds and matched controls, ensuring our claims reflect true family-level robustness rather than an isolated hyperparameter peak. Furthermore, post-hoc calibration effectively repairs confidence estimation without compromising accuracy, and the core approach transfers consistently to secondary backbones like Qwen2.5-VL-3B. The main result is therefore not that a complex auxiliary mechanism wins, but that objective-aligned direct answer SFT is the strongest robust adaptation family we found for MedFrameQA. By establishing this strong, minimalist baseline, we hope to redirect community focus toward fundamentally robust optimization rather than architectural complexity.

</details>


### [13] [ZMIS-SAM: Segment Anything Model Enhanced with Wavelet Transform for Zooplankton Microscopy Image Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27585)
*Dekun Yuan,Zhongwei Li,Zheng Qiao,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合SAM与小波变换的浮游动物实例分割模型（ZMIS-SAM），在边界、细长肢体等分割方面性能领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM模型在显微图像浮游动物实例分割时表现不佳，原因是缺乏特定领域（浮游动物）的知识，难以准确分割细长附肢和边界。

Method: 提出ZMIS-SAM模型，包括三个创新点：通过ZM-ViT模块（含两个轻量级适配器）增强形态和图像分布建模能力；NFAM模块整合通用与领域特征，提升半透明细长附肢的连续分割效果；WM2FE模块用小波变换恢复高频细节，优化边界部分的完整分割。

Result: 大量实验证明ZMIS-SAM在浮游动物数据集上取得了最优实例分割表现，并且在多个公开跨领域数据集上也有很强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ZMIS-SAM显著提升了浮游动物实例分割的准确率和完整性，并具备良好的跨域适应性。

Abstract: As primary consumers in the marine food chain, zooplankton play a crucial role in maintaining marine ecological balance. However, the Segment Anything Model (SAM) exhibits limited performance in microscopic image instance segmentation due to its lack of zooplankton-specific domain knowledge. To address these challenges, we propose a novel instance segmentation model based on SAM and wavelet transform (ZMIS-SAM), effectively tackling issues such as inaccurate classification, discontinuous segmentation of slender appendages, and incomplete boundary segmentation. Our framework incorporates three core innovations: ZM-ViT enhances SAM's capability to model zooplankton morphology and image intensity distributions through two lightweight adapters, the Neighboring Feature Aggregation Module (NFAM) improves continuous segmentation of semi-transparent slender appendages by integrating general-purpose and domain-specific features, and the Wavelet-based Multi-scale Multi-directional Feature Enhancement (WM2FE) module effectively recovers high-frequency details to refine boundary segmentation completeness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ZMIS-SAM achieves state-of-the-art instance segmentation performance on the zooplankton dataset and exhibits strong generalization capability across multiple public cross-domain datasets.

</details>


### [14] [MeshFM: 2D Features Are All You Need for 3D Shape Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27592)
*Jinfan Zhou,Richard Liu,Itai Lang,Rana Hanocka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeshFM是一种高效的前馈框架，将2D视觉基础模型中的特征迁移至3D输入，无需3D标注即可学习强大3D特征，在多个下游任务表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D任务常依赖大量3D注释，而2D基础模型已具备丰富特征。作者希望利用现有2D视觉模型的知识，减少3D特征学习中的标注成本。

Method: 提出MeshFM，采用两阶段策略：首先仅通过2D特征监督优化3D特征场，然后训练前馈网络回归该特征场，全程无需3D标注与测试时优化，并能适配不同3D任务。

Result: 经实验证明，仅用2D监督训练的MeshFM在分割、对应、形变等多项3D任务上，与显式3D监督方法效果相当，且模型对极端旋转具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MeshFM展现了利用2D基础模型特征进行3D特征学习的巨大潜力，为无需3D标注的高效3D视觉学习提供了全新思路。

Abstract: We present MeshFM, an efficient feedforward framework for extracting rich features from 3D inputs. Our method distills 2D features from visual foundation models into 3D. We train a feedforward network to directly predict 3D features without requiring optimization during inference. The approach utilizes a two-stage training strategy. First, we optimize a feature field in 3D using only 2D feature supervision. Second, we train a network to regress this feature field. The entire procedure requires no 3D annotation, instead relying on the powerful information in 2D foundation models. We demonstrate that our learned features can be immediately applied to downstream tasks, including part segmentation, dense correspondence, and mesh deformation. Extensive experiments show that MeshFM, trained solely with 2D supervision, performs on par with methods trained explicitly with 3D supervision, even without task-specific fine-tuning. Moreover, our model is trained to be robust to extreme rotations of the input objects. Project page: https://threedle.github.io/MeshFM/

</details>


### [15] [MPIE-Bench: Benchmarking Anatomically Plausible Multi-Person Interaction Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27616)
*Jiajia Lin,Mingxuan Du,Tuowen Zhou,Benfeng Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 当前文本到图像和个性化编辑模型在生成单人高保真图像效果很好，但对多人物、复杂互动（如拥抱、抬举、缠斗）场景存在严重失败。本文提出了新的基准MPIE-Bench和多人与肢体接触评测指标MPIE-Eval，更好地评估并量化这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像任务已能较好处理单人图像生成，但在多人互动、肢体接触等复杂情景下表现很差。现有评测标准和视觉语言模型（VLM）打分不能准确反映这些问题，导致实际质量与自动打分系统的“高分”不符。因此需要新的评测工具和基准来真正衡量模型在复杂互动场景中的表现。

Method: 1. 构建MPIE-Bench基准集：包括2,500个由视频采样得到的编辑三元组，涵盖了405个场景、14类互动动作和4个肢体接触密度等级。2. 提出MPIE-Eval评价标准：基于固定的公开多人体网格重建模型，在“解剖结构完备性”（Anatomy）和“互动接触精度”（Interaction）两个新维度分别计算得分，直观量化肢体结构与接触合理性。3. 对比10个编辑器和VLM-as-a-judge系统的打分一致性和有效性，通过人工五人打分验证新标准与人类认知的一致性。

Result: 在10个主流文本到图像编辑器实验中，基于网格的Anatomy最高得分为0.65，Interaction最高为0.72，即没有编辑器能在两维都表现优异。而基于VLM的自动检查清单给同样的图像打分通常高于0.95，存在明显脱离实际。五人组人工打分研究进一步验证MPIE-Eval两维得分与人类判断更加一致，并在权重和阈值消融实验下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 现有VLM自动评测工具对多人互动图片的解剖结构和接触合理性把控很差，容易产生高分误判。MPIE-Bench和MPIE-Eval两个新工具能够更细致精确地捕捉并量化这些关键的几何与解剖错误，为后续模型改进和研究提供了必要基准和评估手段。

Abstract: Text-to-image and personalized editing models now synthesize high-fidelity single-subject images with ease. Yet placing multiple named people into shared contact actions such as embrace, carry, or grapple still exposes major failures: fused limbs, invented extremities, and interpenetrating bodies. Existing evaluations largely overlook these anatomical and geometric issues, and VLM-as-a-judge checklists often saturate on Interaction while the errors remain obvious to humans. We introduce MPIE-Bench, a 2,500-sample benchmark of video-mined editing triplets spanning 405 scenes, 14 interaction categories, and four contact densities (C0-C3). We also propose MPIE-Eval, whose two new axes score contact-time geometry from a frozen public multi-person mesh reconstruction. Anatomy asks whether every human-like mass is explained by a complete set of reconstructed bodies, and Interaction asks whether the penetration and surface distance between those bodies match the contact the instruction asked for. Across ten editors, mesh Anatomy tops out at 0.65 and mesh Interaction at 0.72 on two different models, so no single editor is strong on both, while VLM checklists rate the same images above 0.95. A five-rater study confirms that both axes track human judgement more closely than a zero-shot VLM judge, and the rankings hold under ablation of every weight and threshold.

</details>


### [16] [MedXplore: Towards Reliable and Unbiased Generalized Category Discovery in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27620)
*Jianwei He,Kailin Lyu,Junhao Dong,Long Xiao,Wenjie Hou,Jingze Lu,Di Wu,Lin Shu,Jie Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MedXplore框架，针对医学影像中的开放类别发现问题，通过感知与决策两级优化，实现了更鲁棒和无偏的类别发现，有效提升了分类准确率并降低了旧类别误判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数深度学习医学影像分析方法依赖大量标注数据且假设类别集合封闭，这与实际临床环境不符；而开放世界下的新类别发现（GCD）在医学影像领域极少被探索。

Method: 提出MedXplore统一框架，感知层采用频域自适应注意与一致性机制（FAAC），通过全频带滤波和全局-局部能量对比增强局部异常信号；决策层利用自适应余弦角度间隔（ACAM），结合语义难度和特征置信度动态调整聚类间隔，提升类内紧致性与类间区分度。

Result: 该方法在多个医学影像基准上获得了平均8.5%的准确率提升。在Kvasir数据集上，旧类别误判率从14.50%降至0.80%，显示出在强‘旧-新’类别混淆下的卓越鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MedXplore在医学影像类别开放发现任务中，有效提升了异常/病变敏感性、类别表征能力和泛化性。具有实际临床推广应用潜力。

Abstract: Deep learning has shown strong potential in medical image analysis, but most existing methods rely on large-scale annotations and a closed-world assumption that rarely holds in clinical practice. Although Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) has advanced rapidly on natural images, it remains underexplored in medical imaging. To address this issue, we propose MedXplore, a unified framework for reliable and unbiased medical GCD, optimizing from both perceptual and decision levels. Specifically, at the perceptual level, taking a frequency domain perspective, Frequency-SNR Adaptive Attention and Consistency (FAAC) performs learnable full-spectrum filtering and global-local energy contrast activation to not only highlight local abnormal signals relative to the global context, but also provide reliable semantic anchors for patch consistency learning. At the decision level, Adaptive Cosine-Angular Margin (ACAM) adjusts angular margins using semantic difficulty and feature confidence to balance intra-class compactness and inter-class separability. Together, the two modules improve lesion-sensitive representation learning and mitigate old-class bias. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show an average \textbf{8.5\%} gain in \textit{All} accuracy over the strongest competing methods. On Kvasir, MedXplore reduces false-old errors from 14.50\% to 0.80\%, demonstrating strong robustness under severe old-new ambiguity.

</details>


### [17] [BlindPSNR: A No-Reference Fidelity Predictor for Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27628)
*Mingzhe Lyu,Jinqiang Cui,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法BlindPSNR，在没有参考图像的情况下预测低光图像增强后的PSNR，有效提升了参数自动选择的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前低光图像增强方法的参数通常固定，难以应对不同场景，手动调参既费时又不实用。自动参数选择通常依赖PSNR，但多数场景下缺少参考图像。现有无参考图像质量评价（NR-IQA）方法不适用于信号保真度评估，导致自动选择准确率极低。

Method: 作者提出BlindPSNR，一个轻量级无参考神经网络，利用增强图像与原始低光图像的窗口交叉注意力机制，通过异方差回归估算图像增强后的PSNR。

Result: BlindPSNR在参数自动选择上达到89.5%的top-1准确率（远高于标量回归基线的54.4%），并将平均损失从1.62 dB降低至0.026 dB，对新数据集也具备良好泛化能力（SRCC=0.61-0.67）。

Conclusion: BlindPSNR突破了无参考下难以有效评估增强效果的难题，为低光图像增强提供了高效且实用的参数选择工具，推动了相关应用发展。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) methods involve tunable parameters that are typically fixed, often leading to performance degradation when applied across scenes. Manually selecting the best configuration, however, can be time-consuming and not always practical. Peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) is the natural fidelity criterion for automating parameter selection, yet it requires a ground-truth reference that is typically unavailable. To our knowledge, no learning-based method addresses no-reference PSNR prediction for low-light image enhancement; the natural surrogate, no-reference image quality assessment (NR-IQA), targets perceptual quality rather than signal fidelity, and all seven baselines we test achieve 0% top-1 selection accuracy on our benchmark. With paired training data, the ground-truth PSNR is analytically computable, providing exact supervision without a separate teacher network. Building on this, we propose BlindPSNR, a lightweight no-reference network that fuses the enhanced image with the degraded low-light input via windowed cross-attention and estimates PSNR through heteroscedastic regression. While a scalar-regression baseline achieves top-1 accuracy of 54.4%, BlindPSNR raises this to 89.5% with regret dropping from 1.62 dB to 0.026 dB, and generalizes to unseen datasets (SRCC = 0.61-0.67).

</details>


### [18] [4DHumanDiff: Direct Text-to-4DGS Generation for Consistent 360-Degree Dynamic Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27634)
*Renlong Wu,Haoran Chen,Yuxiang Wei,Xiaowei Jin,Wangmeng Zuo,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的扩散框架4DHumanDiff，可以从文本描述直接生成高质量、具有360度可视性的动态人类资产，效率高且一致性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过先生成视频再重建4D表示，过程昂贵且结果常有几何不完整或视角不一致的问题。因此需要直接高效地生成动态4D人类资产的新方法。

Method: 4DHumanDiff基于扩散模型，使用3D U-Net骨干、时序注意力机制，直接在4D Gaussian Splatting空间中端到端地生成动态人体，无需视频预生成及逐场景重建。同时，作者构建了包含6万对高质量数据的大规模文本到4DGS数据集，并引入二维正则和训练免4D插值来提升质量与运动流畅性。

Result: 4DHumanDiff可在一分钟内生成一致性好的360度动态人体，相较竞品提升了时间一致性和多视角一致性，推理时间减少超过10倍。

Conclusion: 4DHumanDiff为从文本生成4D动态人体资产提供了高效、新颖且高质量的解决方案，适合需要高一致性和实时性的应用场景。

Abstract: Generating high-quality 360-degree dynamic human assets from text prompts is challenging. Existing methods usually synthesize monocular or multi-view videos first and then fit a 4D representation, which is expensive and often causes incomplete geometry or view-inconsistent renderings. We present 4DHumanDiff, a diffusion framework that directly generates dynamic humans represented by 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) from text prompts. By modeling the structured 4D representation space end-to-end, 4DHumanDiff avoids video pre-generation and per-scene reconstruction, making it better suited for view-consistent and temporally coherent asset generation. The model uses a 3D U-Net backbone with temporal attention for motion-aware generation. We further construct a large-scale text-to-4DGS dataset with 60,000 high-quality pairs, and introduce 2D regularization and training-free 4D interpolation to improve rendering quality and motion smoothness. Experiments show that 4DHumanDiff generates consistent 360-degree dynamic humans within one minute, achieves better temporal and multi-view consistency, and reduces inference time by more than 10x.

</details>


### [19] [MMOOC: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Out-of-Context Evaluation in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27637)
*Wenjie Zhu,Yabin Zhang,Wenjun Zeng,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MMOOC大规模基准，用于全面评估多模态大模型（MLLMs）在移位语境下的拒答与鲁棒性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前多模态大模型在非理想语境下表现不佳，现有基准忽略了一部分可回答但语境移位的情况，同时覆盖的语境移位类型有限，因此需要更全面的评测方法。

Method: 构建了包含超过4.1万组图像-问题对的MMOOC基准，涵盖回答性与不可回答性情况、三种问题格式、八类语境移位类型和六种视觉场景，数据通过大模型过滤及人工校验。评测指标包括准确率、拒答率，以及基于LLM的判断指标。

Result: 实验显示，当前的MLLMs在移位语境下仍难以平衡回答与拒答能力，通过后训练（Post-training）可提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MMOOC为MLLM领域提供了更细致的评测工具，揭示了模型在移位语境下的局限，并为未来优化方向提供了参考。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on a wide range of vision-language tasks, but often fail under imperfect or shifted contexts. A reliable MLLM should refuse truly out-of-context (OOC) questions with subject-level context shifts while still answering shifted in-context (Shifted IC) questions with non-subject context shifts. Existing benchmarks mainly target OOC or visually unanswerable questions, but overlook answerable Shifted IC cases and cover limited OOC shifts. To fill this gap, we present MMOOC, a large-scale benchmark for evaluating refusal and robust answering abilities of MLLMs. MMOOC contains over 41K image-question pairs, including answerable Shifted IC cases and unanswerable OOC cases, spanning three question formats, eight shift types and six visual scenarios, with data quality ensured through MLLM-based filtering and human verification. We evaluate model responses using Accuracy and Refusal Rate, and further introduce an LLM-as-a-Judge metric to assess the correctness of model reasoning. Experiments on diverse MLLMs show that current models still struggle to balance answer-ability and refusal under shifted contexts. We further analyze key failure patterns and show that post-training can improve robustness. MMOOC will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [20] [Learning Color Grading, No Photo Sharing: Federated Aesthetic Preference Learning for Personalized Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27659)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Ziyuan Tao,Jean Julien KNell,Yanrong Chen,Haolan Guo,Xuanhua Yin,Adnan Mahmood,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无须集中用户数据、能在用户设备上个性化图像美学增强的联邦学习方法FedPAIE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化图像增强需反映用户个人美学偏好，但采集这样的私人照片和评价并集中处理存在隐私与安全问题；同时，用户反馈稀疏且异质，且设备算力有限。研究目标是在本地设备上根据用户有限反馈实现高效、自然的个性化图像增强。

Method: FedPAIE框架通过多阶段训练：首先在去中心化的条件下训练轻量级的双线索美学评分器；然后在本地小样本上微调为个性化评分器并冻结参数；最后基于评分器引导，结合正则与惩罚项，从未配对本地图像中自适应优化轻量CLUT增强器，保证画面自然度和美学一致性，全流程训练和推理参数均保持轻量。

Result: 在MIT-Adobe FiveK和Flickr-AES等公开数据集上，FedPAIE能够有效实现个性化定制，在满足用户美学偏好与保持图像自然保真之间取得良好平衡，验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: FedPAIE成功将隐私友好的去中心化用户美学学习与高效的个性化图像转换结合，无需用户配对调整数据，在保障隐私的同时带来个性化增强效果。

Abstract: Personalized image enhancement should reflect individual aesthetic taste, yet learning such preferences commonly depends on private photos and ratings that are unsuitable for centralized collection. The task must infer preference from sparse, heterogeneous feedback and translate it into natural-looking color transformations on resource-constrained user devices. We introduce FedPAIE, a federated personalized aesthetic image enhancement framework for user-adaptive color grading without centralizing raw photos or ratings. FedPAIE trains a lightweight dual-cue aesthetic scorer, calibrates it into a personalized scorer on a small local support set, and freezes it to guide regularized adaptation of a lightweight CLUT enhancer from unpaired local photographs. Fidelity constraints and an excess-gap penalty regularize scorer-guided adaptation to limit proxy-score over-optimization while preserving content and natural appearance. Training remains lightweight throughout the pipeline: scorer learning updates at most 0.787M parameters, enhancer adaptation updates 0.265M, and inference retains only a 0.293M-parameter personalized enhancer. Experiments on MIT-Adobe FiveK and Flickr-AES demonstrate effective open-world personalization and a favorable balance between user preference and image fidelity. FedPAIE thus connects decentralized preference learning with efficient personalized image transformation without requiring paired user retouches.

</details>


### [21] [Witness Evidence Portfolios: Single-Prefill Risk Detection for Closed Multimodal Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27667)
*Fexiang Liu,Shiye Wang,Qiang Qiu,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为Witness Evidence Portfolios (WEP)的新方法，用于在推理阶段白盒分析多模态大模型的可视答案风险。WEP通过分层解读视觉证据，提高了模型答案可信度的判定能力，并在多个基准测试中实现可靠性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在生成视觉答案时，仅依靠置信度分数评价结果可靠性，存在难以细致解释模型做出判断的依据的问题。这导致在实际部署时难以决定结果是否需要审核或转交更强模型。缺乏对视觉贡献分布和具体支撑/反对证据的细致剖析，限制了模型推理安全性的提升。

Method: WEP方法在模型白盒内部推理（prefill阶段）过程中，逐层识别和量化视觉特征对候选答案的支持或反对贡献。其核心在于构建两类可解释证据路径：与问题相关的证据来源（evidence provenance）和签名证据集中度（evidence concentration）。通过嵌套组验证方法自动选择更可靠的证据路径，并与置信度融合形成稀疏精炼的证据组合。该方法无需对图像做扰动、解码变更、逆向传播或调用外部验证器。

Result: 在三种MLLMs和四个二分类视觉基准数据集上，WEP平均错误AP提升0.134。全部12组模型-数据集组合均表现为正向提升，且其中10组在图像聚类自助法区间上显著为正。这表明方法具有稳健性和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: WEP无需复杂额外操作，仅利用模型自身的prefill路径，便可以高效白盒解释视觉答案可信性，为多模态大模型在实际应用中风险管理和安全部署提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Reliable deployment of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) requires deciding whether a confident visual answer should be trusted, reviewed, or routed to a stronger system. Confidence scores capture candidate margins, but not where the estimated signed visual readouts associated with those margins come from or how they are distributed. We study inference-time risk detection for closed visual answers using the same white-box prefill path that produces the answer. Witness Evidence Portfolios (WEP) first estimates, layer by layer, which visual contributions support or contradict the predicted candidate. It summarizes these contributions through two interpretable route families: question-related evidence provenance and signed evidence concentration. Nested grouped validation chooses the more reliable family and a sparse top-k route portfolio, which is fused with candidate confidence. WEP needs no image perturbation, decoding change, backward pass, or external verifier. Across three MLLMs and four binary-answer benchmarks, WEP improves mean error AP by 0.134. All 12 model--dataset gains are positive, and image-cluster bootstrap intervals are strictly positive on 10 pairs. WEP targets white-box closed-answer systems and uses a labeled calibration slice.

</details>


### [22] [JigShape: Evaluating Visual-Geometric Reasoning in VLMs through Jigsaw Puzzles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27670)
*Shawn Li,Wei Yang,Jike Zhong,Jiate Li,Jiawei Yang,You Qin,Ryan Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt,Roger Zimmermann,Yue Wang,Zhengzhong Tu,Vicente Ordonez,Mohit Bansal,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的拼图基准测试（\ours），通过插槽和凸起式拼图设计，结合视觉和几何约束，提供更加明确的拼图配对标准，发现现有主流视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在几何推理上表现较差，尤其在拼图规模扩大时表现显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拼图基准测试主要采用规则矩形切割，导致在纹理重复区域容易出现歧义配对，无法准确反映模型的几何推理能力。作者希望通过设计更加严格和贴近真实拼图结构的基准，考察和推动视觉-语言模型在几何约束和视觉内容联合推理方面的能力。

Method: 作者引入了一种带有插槽和凸起互锁结构的新型拼图基准（\ours），通过设置多种拼图难度（从4x4到16x16），构建了95,000个拼图实例，并系统评测了主流零样本VLMs和经过有监督微调的模型在此基准上的表现。

Result: 实验显示，零样本VLMs普遍缺乏几何推理能力，仅有GPT-5.5在最简单的4x4拼图中略高于随机水平，其余模型与随机水平基本无异。即使经过有监督微调的模型，在4x4拼图上可达97%以上的准确率，但一旦增加拼图规模，所有模型效果急剧下降，在12x12时准确率低于5%。

Conclusion: 作者认为现有VLM架构难以应对随拼图规模增长带来的几何约束和组合复杂性问题，提出几何推理可拓展性是视觉-语言模型面临的一个重要开放挑战。

Abstract: Jigsaw puzzle solving requires jointly reasoning about visual content and geometric constraints, yet existing benchmarks use rectangular cuts that create ambiguous ground truth in texture-repeated regions. We introduce \textit{\ours{}}, a benchmark with tab-and-blank interlocking pieces where geometric constraints provide strong local compatibility requirements that, combined with visual content, yield unambiguous ground truth. Across 95K instances at four grid densities (4$\times$4 to 16$\times$16), we find that \textbf{zero-shot VLMs largely lack geometric reasoning}: only one of five frontier models (GPT-5.5) exceeds random baseline on 4$\times$4 puzzles, while all others perform at chance level. While supervised fine-tuning achieves $>$97\% on 4$\times$4, \textbf{all models collapse on larger grids}: GPT-5.5 drops from 70\% to near-random on 8$\times$8, and even fine-tuned models fall below 5\% on 12$\times$12. This ``scaling cliff'' suggests current architectures cannot maintain consistent constraint satisfaction as the number of pieces increases. \ours{} establishes scalable geometric reasoning as an open challenge for vision-language models.

</details>


### [23] [RefineSVG: Visual Feedback-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Image-to-SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27699)
*Shaobo Liu,Feiqiao Mao,Shuaishuai Zhou,Yan Zhan,Weiqi Tan,Zhiqiong Lu,Zhengping Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefineSVG提出了一种让多模态大模型通过视觉反馈自动修正并高质量生成SVG图像的新方法，比现有方法生成的图片更准确、代码更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型生成SVG时依赖一次性推理，无法在生成中修正图像错误，容易产生结构偏移和视觉错误，特别是在复杂图像场景下表现不佳。

Method: 1. 首先进行初步的SVG生成；2. 用外部渲染引擎将SVG代码可视化，并与目标图像比对；3. 得到视觉残差（Diff-Map）反馈给模型，用于下一步的修正；4. 增加SVG相关语义词表，极大减少生成token数量；5. 采用逐步训练策略（监督微调、拒绝采样冷启动、端到端强化学习）优化模型闭环修正能力。

Result: RefineSVG在重建保真度、结构准确性和代码效率方面比现有基线方法表现更优。

Conclusion: RefineSVG利用闭环视觉反馈机制和高效token序列压缩，极大提升了多模态大模型的SVG生成能力，实现了更高的准确率和生成效率。

Abstract: We propose RefineSVG, a single-step closed-loop visual feedback framework that enables multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to perform high-fidelity image-to-SVG generation through self-correction. Existing MLLM-based approaches rely on single-pass open-loop inference, where the model receives visual input only once and must generate thousands of SVG code tokens without intermediate verification. This paradigm inevitably leads to geometric drift, error accumulation, and visual hallucination on complex images. RefineSVG overcomes this limitation by invoking an external rendering engine after an initial SVG generation pass to compare the rendered output against the target image. The comparison yields a multi-dimensional visual residual map (Diff-Map) that is fed back to the model as a ReAct-style correction signal, driving a targeted correction step. To support this render-observe-correct interaction, we further introduce an SVG-oriented semantic vocabulary that compresses token sequences by over 52%. A progressive training pipeline spanning supervised fine-tuning, rejection-sampling cold-start data construction, and end-to-end agentic reinforcement learning aligns the model with closed-loop visual correction. Extensive experiments show that RefineSVG consistently outperforms existing baselines in reconstruction fidelity, structural accuracy, and code efficiency.Code is available at https://github.com/liuxiaobo66/RefineSVG.

</details>


### [24] [Articulated Object Reconstruction from Rest-State Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27749)
*Daeun Lee,Jaeah Lee,Woosung Kim,Haebeom Jung,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种只需单一静止状态即可重建具有关节结构的物体方法，利用多种先验和生成模型克服缺乏运动信息的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可动物体重建方法通常依赖物体多个不同运动状态下的观测数据。然而，在实际应用中，往往只能获取到物体的单一闭合（静止）状态，如何从静止状态恢复关节结构和几何形态成为亟需解决的问题。

Method: 1. 用显式三维网格作为中间表示，融合不同视觉-语言与分割模型输出，得到空间一致的物体零件结构。2. 为估计关节参数，在没有运动观测的情况下，借助视频扩散模型生成物体运动假设，并通过几何一致性验证假设的可行性。

Result: 本方法能有效实现对物体零件的精确分解和物理合理的运动结构重建，其性能与基于运动观测（重建/生成/多模型）的主流方法相比有竞争力。

Conclusion: 本工作首次在只给定单一静止状态下，实现了具有关节结构物体的高质量重建，为数字孪生与交互场景提供了新思路，也为缺乏运动观测的实际问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Building interactive digital twins requires recovering both 3D geometry and the kinematic structures that govern how objects articulate. Yet existing methods for articulated object reconstruction require explicitly observable motion from multiple articulation states. We introduce a rest-state formulation that reconstructs articulated objects from a single closed configuration, an inherently ill-posed setting where geometry, semantics, and motion priors compensate for the absence of motion cues. Our framework adopts an explicit mesh as an intermediate representation for cross-model verification and fusion, reconciling noisy outputs from vision-language and segmentation models into spatially consistent part structures. To estimate joint parameters without observed motion, we use a video diffusion model to synthesize articulation hypotheses and validate them through geometric consistency. Our approach achieves accurate part decomposition and physically plausible articulation, performing competitively with motion-observing reconstruction-based, generation-based, and modular pretrained-model baselines.

</details>


### [25] [Calibrate Before Reason: Robust Visual Token Reduction against Semantic Drift in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27700)
*Jiasheng Li,Zhong Ji,Yan Zhang,Huihui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效且训练无关的视觉token压缩方法CaRe，在极大减少视觉token的情况下，依然能很好保持原有的语义准确性和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大视觉-语言模型（VLMs）推理时视觉token序列很长，导致推理速度慢。现有token裁剪/压缩方法只顾提升效率，未重视精简后语义是否与原始一致，经常会造成误删关键信息，导致模型理解偏移（semantic drift）。

Method: 提出“先校准再推理”（Calibrate Before Reason）原则，研发了CaRe框架，无需再训练，通过两大模块实现：（1）扰动鲁棒的校准锚定，选出对模型具有鲁棒影响力的锚点token；（2）置信门控token校准，将未选token里的置信信息融合进锚点token，确保语义不丢失。

Result: 在多种VLM模型和基准测试集上，CaRe在减少94.4%视觉token的同时，仍保持96.4%的原始性能，推理速度提升至2.3倍，显著优于现有主流token裁剪技术。

Conclusion: CaRe能极大提升VLMs推理效率，并有效减缓信息丢失与语义漂移问题，适合高效部署大视觉-语言模型。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) suffer from prohibitive inference overhead due to long sequences of visual tokens. However, existing visual token reduction methods mainly improve efficiency by pruning or compressing redundant tokens without examining whether the resulting representation remains semantically consistent with the original representation. Mapping the original N-token visual sequence to K tokens may discard, dilute, or misassign critical visual cues, triggering severe semantic drift that deviates the VLM's understanding. In this paper, we first introduce the principle of 'Calibrate Before Reason' to visual token reduction and propose CaRe, a training-free robust framework that calibrates compact visual representations before reasoning to preserve semantic fidelity in VLMs. CaRe consists of two mutually complementary modules: 1) Perturbation-Robust Calibration Anchoring, which identifies calibration anchors with stable model-side influence under multi-directional perturbations; 2) Confidence-Gated Token Calibration, which extracts reliable calibration signals from unselected tokens and injects them into anchors. Extensive evaluations across diverse VLM architectures and benchmarks verify that CaRe outperforms state-of-the-art token reduction baselines. While pruning 94.4% of visual tokens, our method retains 96.4% of the original full-token performance, delivering up to 2.30 times faster end-to-end inference speed relative to unpruned vanilla models.

</details>


### [26] [Filling the Pareto-Optimal Front for Affordance Segmentation on Embedded Devices Using RGB-D Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28293)
*Edoardo Ragusa,Giovanni Paolo Canuti,Simone Lugani,Rodolfo Zunino,Paolo Gastaldo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对可穿戴机器人中的affordance分割，提出两种将深度（Depth）信息与RGB数据结合的方法，以适应受限硬件的实际应用，并在真实数据集上验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RGB的affordance分割在小型嵌入式硬件上受限，且深度数据的补充潜力未被充分利用。实际应用中，对低功耗、实时性和高适应性的需求强烈，因而亟需优化结合RGB和Depth信息的方法。

Method: （1）提出硬件感知神经架构搜索（NAS）的改进版本，设计新的搜索空间用于集成Depth信息，生成适合小型设备的网络。
（2）提出微调方法，在网络中加入预处理层，融合Depth与RGB数据，使之适配主流架构。
上述方法均兼顾模型性能与硬件实施的可行性。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集上进行实验，所提方法优于现有方法，在性能与硬件资源需求之间实现帕累托最优平衡。原型基于Jetson Nano板与RealSense RGB-D相机，能实现在智能手机电池等级能源预算内的实时推理。

Conclusion: 本文方法能高效结合RGB和Depth信息，适配现代便携硬件，实现高效、低能耗的affordance分割，为可穿戴机器人实际应用提供了有效技术路径。

Abstract: While depth sensors have the potential to complement RGB data for affordance segmentation in wearable robots, their usage seems to remain underexplored. The paper proposes two approaches: a reformulated version of hardware-aware neural architecture search, endowed with a newly designed search space to integrate depth (D) information into small-sized deep networks, and a dedicated fine-tuning approach, including a preprocessing layer to merge depth information with RGB data and make it compatible with conventional architectures. In both cases, those methods aim to generate solutions that benefit from modern (portable) hardware accelerators and overcome existing tiny-like approaches, which often fail to tackle critical scenarios due to the severe constraints set by the supporting hardware. Extensive experiments on a pair of real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method as compared with existing solutions. The approach presented in the paper generates, in most cases, solutions that identify the Pareto optimal front to balance generalization performance and hardware requirements. The paper also describes the supporting prototype, including a Jetson Nano board and a RealSense RGB-D camera. When considering the energy profile of the device, the overall system can attain real-time performances within an energy budget that is compatible with standard batteries, such as those used in smartphones.

</details>


### [27] [PrintAnything: Learning an Intermediate Representation for 3D printing G-code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27729)
*Sangmin Hong,Daniel Sungho Jung,Heewon Kim,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，可以直接从点云生成可用于3D打印的G-code，无需经过网格重建，避免了传统方法因网格缺陷导致的打印失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D打印流程通常要求防水网格作为输入，而实际很多3D形状以点云形式存在。从点云重建网格常导致几何错误、拓扑不一致，这些很难修复且会引发打印失败。因此，亟需一种能跳过网格环节、直接利用点云的3D打印方案。

Method: 作者提出了PrintAnything框架，采用分层点投影策略，将无结构点云转化为与3D打印分层操作一致的2D切片表现。在此基础上，进一步设计了“G-plan map”，该2D紧凑表示综合了占据、区域及流动等信息，精确反映3D打印路径的几何和挤出属性，可直接生成G-code。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能准确地从点云直接生成可打印的G-code，实现了无需网格重建的3D打印全流程。相关代码已开源。

Conclusion: 本文方法打破了点云到3D打印必须依赖网格重建的局限，提供了一种高效、稳健、实用的无网格3D打印新途径。

Abstract: Point clouds are one of the most fundamental and widely used 3D representations, serving as the most basic geometric representation of 3D shapes. Nevertheless, most existing 3D printing pipelines require a watertight mesh as input, preventing the direct use of point clouds for fabrication. A common workaround is to reconstruct meshes from point clouds; however, the resulting meshes often contain geometric artifacts, such as incorrect faces or topological inconsistencies, that are difficult to repair and may lead to printing failures. To overcome these limitations, we propose PrintAnything, a novel framework that learns to produce executable 3D printing G-code directly from 3D point clouds without requiring mesh reconstruction. To enable point clouds to serve as direct input for slice-wise toolpath generation, we introduce a slice-wise point projection strategy that transforms unstructured 3D point clouds into slice-aligned 2D representations consistent with layer-by-layer nature of fused deposition modeling in 3D printing. To eliminate mesh dependency and provide a unified representation that bridges point clouds and G-code, we propose Geometric plan (G-plan) map, a compact 2D representation composed of occupancy, region, and flow maps that encode the geometric and extrusion properties required for toolpath synthesis in 3D printing. As a result, our proposed method accurately generates printable G-code directly from point clouds, enabling a practical and fully mesh-free pipeline for 3D printing. The code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/Sangminhong/PrintAnything}{https://github.com/Sangminhong/PrintAnything}.

</details>


### [28] [EgoGVAE: Ego-body Mesh Reconstruction via Guided Variational Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27755)
*Jaehun Jung,Wonjun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种能够仅通过头部姿态重建全身网格的新方法，速度较以往方法提高了50倍，并在基准数据集上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着头戴设备和智能眼镜的普及，单凭头部姿态推断全身动作变得十分必要。然而，当前主流的方法基于耗时的扩散生成模型，效率低，不适用于实时应用。该研究旨在解决全身不可观测部分动作还原的效率和实用性问题。

Method: 作者设计了一个变分自编码器（VAE）作为“引导网络”，以全身姿态作为输入，将生成的潜变量分布引导到与仅用头部姿态训练的“head-to-motion网络”一致，使得单凭头部姿态采样出的隐变量可以有效解码生成自然合理的全身姿态；该方法为一步采样，极大提升了推理速度。

Result: 实验结果表明，提出的方法在公开基准数据集上重建效果更优，并且比扩散模型类方法推理速度提升超过50倍。

Conclusion: 论文验证了所提方法在全身动作恢复任务上的有效性和高效率，为基于头部姿态的全身网格重建提供了新的高效方法。

Abstract: We address the problem of recovering the full-body mesh from only the head pose. This task has become essential for various applications based on head-mounted devices or smart glasses. The challenge of this task lies in estimating the pose information of unobserved body parts based solely on a single joint (i.e., head) trajectory. Several studies have begun to adopt head-conditioned generative models, however, such previous methods are costly and time-consuming due to the diffusion-based iterative process. As an alternative, we propose a simple yet novel method that leverages the latent space of the guidance network, which is designed as a variational autoencoder taking full-body poses as inputs. By enforcing latent distributions of this guidance network and our head-to-motion network to be similar, latent features sampled from the 'guided' distribution, i.e., distribution learned in our head-to-motion network, can be reliably decoded for natural representations of full-body poses even only with the head pose. One important advantage of the proposed method is that one-step sampling scheme achieves remarkably fast inference (more than 50 times faster) compared to diffusion-based approaches. Experimental results on benchmark datasets show that the proposed method efficiently improves the performance of ego-body mesh reconstruction.

</details>


### [29] [DS@GT ARC at ImageCLEFmedical 2026: Architectural Diversity for Concept Detection and Foundation-Model Scaling for Caption Prediction in Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27763)
*Bowen Wang,Youwen Zhang,Ritesh Mehta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DS@GT团队在ImageCLEFmedical Caption 2026挑战赛上的解决方案，在ROCOv2数据集的两个任务（医学影像UMLS概念识别与影像描述生成）中取得了领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像的自动解读与理解一直是人工智能领域的难题，挑战在于多样的影像内容与标签稀缺，作者希望通过模型集成与创新训练策略提升稀有医学概念的检测准确率，以及降低影像描述生成的门槛和成本。

Method: 在概念检测任务（任务1）中，作者采用ConvNeXt-V2、BiomedCLIP ViT-B/16和DenseNet-169三模型后融合，并引入正则化的“Honest Threshold Tuning”防止验证集过拟合。另有零训练成本的基于冻结BiomedCLIP嵌入的KNN检索方案。在影像描述生成任务（任务2）中，尝试了大语言模型Gemma-3 27B微调、BLIP详细微调与自定义合并、以及MedGemma-4B的零样本推理。

Result: 任务1融合模型F1首次排名第一（主指标0.5790，次指标0.9657），KNN检索方案以极低成本基本持平（主指标0.5780，次指标0.9599）。任务2最佳方案Gemma-3 27B微调，官方提交排名第三（0.3571），其它方案表现亦不俗。

Conclusion: 多模型融合和创新的阈值调优方法在医学影像概念识别中取得显著提升，低成本无训练方法效果接近最优。多元模型可覆盖不同规模与成本需求，为实际医学AI工具开发提供参考。

Abstract: We describe the DS@GT submissions to the ImageCLEFmedical Caption 2026 challenge, which continues a long-running benchmark on the ROCOv2 dataset with two tracks: Concept Detection (Task 1), assigning UMLS Concept Unique Identifiers (CUIs) to radiology images, and Caption Prediction (Task 2), generating natural-language captions. For Task 1, our primary submission was a three-way late-fusion ensemble of ConvNeXt-V2, BiomedCLIP ViT-B/16, and DenseNet-169 with a regularized ''Honest Threshold Tuning'' procedure designed to avoid validation overfitting on rare concepts; this submission ranked first on the official submission with a primary $F_1$ of $0.5790$ and a secondary $F_1$ of $0.9657$. In parallel, we submitted a training-free KNN retrieval pipeline over frozen BiomedCLIP embeddings, which reached a primary $F_1$ of $0.5780$ and a secondary $F_1$ of $0.9599$-essentially matching the fine-tuned ensemble on the primary track at a fraction of the cost. For Task 2, our submissions included a fine-tuned Gemma-3 27B model (overall $0.3571$, ranking third in the official submission), a fully fine-tuned BLIP pipeline with custom Vizwins merging ($0.3564$), and a zero-shot MedGemma-4B run with a PubMed-style prompt ($0.3186$), spanning a wide range of model scales and training costs. Code: https://github.com/dsgt-arc/imageclef-caption-2026.

</details>


### [30] [Private Face Recognition Training Dataset Publication via Identity-Decoupled and Geometry-Preserving Face Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27764)
*Shuhuan Chen,Xiangyu Zhu,Weisong Zhao,Siran Peng,Tianshuo Zhang,Haoyuan Zhang,Haichao Shi,Xiao-Yu Zhang,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文旨在解决人脸识别模型训练数据隐私问题，通过提出一种既保护隐私又保持识别有效性的代理数据发布方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别训练数据的发布容易泄露个人身份信息。现有方法通过发布受保护的代理数据替代真实人脸，但难以平衡识别性能与隐私保护：过度保护会削弱类结构，不足保护又容易被追溯到真实身份。本文认为，症结在于没有区分对识别有用的特征和会导致身份泄露的特征。

Method: 提出了Private Face Distillation方法，通过正交几何保持分离代理身份和原始身份，同时保存用于识别学习的几何结构。使用关系拓扑对齐技术，确保代理身份之间的关系适合识别训练。

Result: 在多种数据域下实验，所提方法在IJB-C监控数据集上比基线方法提高了3.94%的准确率，并显著降低了源身份的可链接性。

Conclusion: 发布人脸识别训练集时应当在去除可追溯的身份信息的同时尽量保留用于训练的代理身份几何结构。

Abstract: Publishing private face recognition~(FR) training datasets is privacy-sensitive because faces expose identity information. Private FR training dataset publication mitigates this risk by releasing protected proxies as substitutes for private training faces. However, training FR models with such data introduces an identity paradox: \emph{the identity cues that make released faces useful for recognition supervision are also the cues that make them linkable to real individuals.} A protected face should be decoupled from the original identity, yet still behave as a reliable identity sample for training. Removing these cues too aggressively may destroy the class structure needed for recognition learning, whereas preserving them too faithfully may increase source-identity linkability. We argue that this paradox stems from conflating source-aligned identity semantics with recognition-useful proxy identity geometry. The former should be suppressed to reduce linkage to private individuals, while the latter should be preserved for FR learning. Based on this insight, we propose \textbf{Private Face Distillation}, an identity-decoupling and geometry-preserving framework. It uses Orthogonal Geometry Preservation to construct decoupled proxy identities from private identity representations while maintaining hyperspherical geometry, and Relational Topology Alignment to preserve identity relations for recognition learning. Experiments across multiple domain-shifted FR scenarios show that Private Face Distillation achieves stronger utility than the evaluated publication baselines. On IJB-C surveillance, it improves $\mathrm{TAR}@\mathrm{FAR}{=}1\text{e-}{3}$ by 3.94\% over the baseline while reducing source-identity linkability. These results suggest that private FR training dataset publication should decouple source-identity correspondence while preserving proxy identity geometry.

</details>


### [31] [CXR-Retrieve: Compositional Text-to-Image Retrieval in Chest Radiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27779)
*Tomer Erez,Moshe Kimhi,Chaim Baskin,Ehud Rivlin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出了CXR-Retrieve基准和一种标签感知对比微调方法，用于提升胸部X光文本到图像检索在合成性查询（如合并和否定病理）上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的大型胸部X光图像数据库多只配有自由文本报告，缺乏结构化的临床标签，使得以精准临床条件（特别是带有合并和否定约束的查询）进行图像检索变得困难。目前医学视觉-语言模型主要针对报告-图像匹配优化，难以直接处理此类精细检索需求。

Method: 提出CXR-Retrieve基准，包含5,159张测试图像和145条组合性文本查询，对检索相关性以是否满足所有病理约束为标准。进一步提出了一种标签感知的对比微调目标，使图像-文本对之间能更好地对齐病理标签（包括阴性标签），并显式区分有矛盾的样本对。

Result: 模型在CXR-CLIP基础上微调后，实现了Precision@5在双病理合取查询上提升8.5个百分点，在否定类查询上提升22个百分点，明显优于原有模型。

Conclusion: 高可靠性胸部X光图像检索不仅要求识别提及的病理，还需正确解析其临床断言（如合并/否定），为相关场景设计和训练专门的目标函数是必要的。

Abstract: Large chest radiography archives are difficult to search because most studies are paired only with free-text reports rather than structured clinical annotations. Vision-language models offer a natural interface for text-to-image retrieval, but current biomedical models are primarily optimized for report-to-image matching rather than for satisfying short clinical search queries. This creates an objective mismatch: a model may retrieve images related to words in the query while failing to satisfy the full clinical constraint, especially for conjunctions and negations such as ``atelectasis and no pneumonia.''
  We introduce CXR-Retrieve, a structured benchmark for compositional chest X-ray text-to-image retrieval. The benchmark contains 5,159 test images from the official test-split of MIMIC-CXR-JPG and 145 textual queries spanning single and conjunction findings, both positive and negative. Relevance is defined by whether a retrieved image satisfies all asserted pathology constraints, rather than by whether it matches a paired report.
  We further propose a label-aware contrastive fine-tuning objective for clinical retrieval. Our method attracts image-text pairs with compatible asserted pathology constraints, including shared confirmed absences, while explicitly repelling contradictory pairs. Starting from the in-domain CXR-CLIP checkpoint, our method improves Precision@5 over CXR-CLIP by 8.5 percentage points on two-pathology conjunctions and by 22.0 percentage points on negation queries. These results show that reliable chest X-ray retrieval requires training objectives that model not only which findings are mentioned, but also how they are clinically asserted.

</details>


### [32] [FDDWAN: A Frequency-Decoupled Diffusion Network for Watermarking Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27800)
*Chunpeng Wang,Yuxin Li,Xiaoyu Wang,Jidong Yang,Suo Gao,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于频域解耦扩散的新型水印攻击网络（FDDWAN），能够更有效地移除隐形水印，并保持较高的图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐形水印移除方法在抑制水印和保持图像感知质量之间存在权衡，难以精确捕捉带水印特征，导致去水印不彻底或图像损伤。

Method: 提出了FDDWAN，该方法分为两个阶段：1）利用小波分解将图像分为低频和高频子带，并针对不同子带采用专门的攻击策略；2）通过残差扩散模型对初步处理后的输出和无水印参考之间的残差建模，只微调频域残差以进一步去除残留水印。

Result: 在CelebA和ImageNet，并针对四种典型水印方式进行了大量实验，FDDWAN在水印移除效果和视觉质量之间取得了比现有方法更优的平衡。

Conclusion: FDDWAN方法在保持原始图像内容的前提下，有效提升了隐形水印移除性能，比传统和学习型攻击方法表现更好。

Abstract: Existing invisible watermark removal methods often struggle to accurately capture the watermark-bearing features, leading to an unfavorable trade-off between watermark suppression and perceptual fidelity. In this paper, we propose the Frequency-Decoupled Diffusion Watermark Attack Network (FDDWAN), a coarse-to-fine framework that performs watermark removal through wavelet-domain decomposition and residual diffusion refinement. In the initial stage, the Wavelet-based Frequency-domain Preliminary Attack Module (WFPAM) decomposes the watermarked image into low- and high-frequency subbands and applies frequency-specific attack strategies tailored to their respective contributions to watermark robustness and perceptual quality. In the next stage, the Frequency-domain Residual Diffusion Attack Module (FRDAM) separately models the residual distributions between the preliminarily attacked outputs and the corresponding watermark-free references during training. Rather than reconstructing the entire image, FRDAM selectively refines frequency-domain residuals, directing the diffusion process toward the remaining watermark related discrepancies while minimizing modifications to image content. Extensive experiments on CelebA and ImageNet across four representative watermarking schemes demonstrate that FDDWAN achieves a more favorable trade-off between watermark removal effectiveness and visual fidelity than conventional and learning-based attack methods.

</details>


### [33] [LoMeVQA: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Longitudinal Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27806)
*Zhilin Wu,Zhangkai Ni,Chengmei Yang,Longzhen Yang,Yihang Liu,Ying Wen,Lianghua He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了LoMeVQA数据集，专注于纵向医学视觉问答任务，并展示了现有多模态大模型在该任务上的不足，推出了新模型MedLong-8B显著提升了表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床中病人经常多次影像检查，纵向数据丰富，但目前多模态大模型很少被用于纵向医学视觉推理领域。缺乏相关研究和数据集阻碍了疾病进展与疗效评估的智能化发展。

Method: 作者设计了自动化流程，整合临床病历、医学知识图谱和时间线，生成纵向视觉问答对，涵盖五大任务，并建立大规模数据集LoMeVQA。评测了现有MLLMs性能，并提出了更优的新模型MedLong-8B。

Result: 广泛实验显示通用及医学领域MLLMs在纵向推理上的表现均较差，且存在显著缺陷。新提出的MedLong-8B模型在全部任务上取得最优表现。

Conclusion: LoMeVQA有效促进了纵向医学视觉推理研究，揭示了现有模型的局限，并通过分析和新模型为未来改进指明了方向。

Abstract: In clinical practice, patients often undergo multiple imaging examinations over successive visits, yielding longitudinal data. Modeling such temporal information is crucial for reliable assessment of disease progression and treatment response. However, despite the rapid advancement of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), longitudinal medical visual reasoning remains largely underexplored. To fill this gap, we propose LoMeVQA, a comprehensive benchmark consisting of 206K longitudinal visual question answering (VQA) pairs for temporal medical image analysis. LoMeVQA covers five tasks: progress classification, progress description, progress report generation, differential region grounding, and differential region description. To construct the dataset, we develop an automated pipeline that (1) organizes patient records chronologically, (2) extracts clinically meaningful entities via a medical knowledge graph, and (3) models their temporal evolution to guide large language models in generating high-quality longitudinal VQA pairs. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that both general-purpose and medical-domain MLLMs perform poorly on LoMeVQA, revealing substantial limitations in temporal reasoning. To address these limitations, we introduce MedLong-8B, which achieves state-of-the-art performance across all tasks. Beyond benchmarking, we conduct detailed analyses that uncover key failure modes and shed light on how to improve longitudinal medical visual reasoning. Our data is available at: https://github.com/pepperbubble/LoMeVQA

</details>


### [34] [SPFM-Net: Semantic-Prior-Guided Frequency-Constrained Mamba for Invisible Watermark Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27811)
*Chunpeng Wang,Yanan Shi,Zhiqiu Xia,Jidong Yang,Suo Gao,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPFM-Net提出了一种新颖的不可见水印去除框架，结合语义先验和频率约束，突破现有方法对全球分布水印信号处理的局限，实现高效去水印和高视觉保真度的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印去除方法局限于预定义的信号处理或仅关注局部信息，难以有效处理全球分布的不可见水印信号，导致去除效果与视觉质量之间出现权衡不足。论文旨在解决这一矛盾，提升去除准确性及保真度。

Method: 提出SPFM-Net框架，包含高比例mask破坏空间水印连贯性，利用部分微调的预训练Masked Autoencoder进行语义重构与水印抑制；设计多尺度残差频率特征交互模块，从多个感受野聚合同源水印特征并抑制无关响应；引入轻量级Mamba全局特征建模单元GSFM，进一步分离水印特征并抑制残留；全网络采用多级目标函数联合空间、频率及边缘约束优化。

Result: 在空间域、变换域、正交矩基和深度学习水印的多个代表性方案上，SPFM-Net取得了卓越的去除效果与视觉保真兼顾的表现，优于现有主流方法。

Conclusion: SPFM-Net能更有效地处理全球分布的不可见水印信号，在去除水印效率与感知质量之间实现更优折中，为不可见水印攻击提供新思路。

Abstract: Existing watermark attacks typically rely on predefined signal-processing operations or locally constrained restoration networks, making it difficult to capture the long-range dependencies of globally distributed watermark signals and resulting in an unfavorable trade-off between removal effectiveness and visual fidelity. In this paper, we propose SPFM-Net, a semantic-prior-guided and frequency-constrained Mamba framework for invisible watermark attack. SPFM-Net first employs high-ratio masking to disrupt the spatial coherence of invisible watermark signals, and then utilizes a partially fine-tuned pretrained Masked Autoencoder to reconstruct semantically consistent image from sparse observations while suppressing watermark-related information. A Multi-scale Residual Frequency Feature Interaction module subsequently aggregates watermark-related residual features across multiple receptive fields, while adaptively suppressing responses from watermark-irrelevant regions. To further capture the long-range dependencies of globally distributed watermark signals, a lightweight Mamba-based Global State-space Feature Modeling (GSFM) unit is introduced to separate watermark-related features from natural image content and suppress the remaining watermark traces. In addition, SPFM-Net is optimized using a multi-level objective that jointly imposes spatial-, frequency-, and edge-domain constraints, enabling effective watermark suppression while preserving perceptual quality. Extensive experiments on representative spatial-domain, transform-domain, orthogonal moment-based, and deep learning-based watermarking schemes demonstrate that SPFM-Net achieves a favorable trade-off between watermark attack effectiveness and perceptual fidelity.

*Lei Yang,Xinze Liu,Dayan Wu,Ding Wang,Hengjie Zhu,Zihao Zhang,Tianzhu Hu,Hanqi Wu,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在图像描述生成过程中出现“幻觉对象”（即描述中出现图像中并不存在的物体）的问题，提出了一种基于内在绑定签名（IGS）的诊断与干预方法——Verifier-Guided Decoding（VGD），能够有效降低对象幻觉，同时保持对真实物体的描述覆盖率。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate objects that are absent from an image. Despite recent progress, existing mitigation methods still lack reliable object-level grounding diagnostics and therefore tend to apply coarse-grained interventions, which can impair visual understanding, shorten responses, and reduce coverage of genuinely grounded objects. The key challenge is thus to detect, during generation, whether each emerging object mention is supported by reliable visual evidence, so that hallucination can be mitigated selectively. Yet output confidence reflects next-token plausibility rather than visual support, allowing language priors to make absent objects appear certain. We show that the missing diagnostic evidence is encoded in an Intrinsic Grounding Signature (IGS), a distributed signed attention pattern that remains informative for such confident hallucinations. Based on IGS, we propose Verifier-Guided Decoding (VGD), a decoding framework in which a lightweight verifier examines each emerging object mention, rolls back the KV cache when the mention is identified as high risk, suppresses the object and its synonyms, and regenerates the affected continuation. Because VGD intervenes only on object mentions identified as high risk, it reduces object hallucination while preserving the model's original visual understanding and grounded object coverage. Experiments on CHAIR and AMBER-G show that VGD achieves state-of-the-art object hallucination reduction: at @rec90, it cuts AMBER-G CHAIR by 43.6\% while retaining 99.6\% of grounded-object coverage, and reduces CHAIR-MSCOCO CHAIR$_i$/CHAIR$_s$ by 37.0\%/30.4\% without shortening captions.

</details>
